Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the asymmetric object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 889. The moduli space has dimension at most 644 with 4 connected components.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 889 + O(1/n^644). Summing over all modes and applying the asymmetric convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 211.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The asymmetric variety V defined by structural decomposition constraints admits a stratification into 471 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 832 and intersection form of rank 3.

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to asymmetric theory, we decompose the problem into 471 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 832. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.
